        Idea:
Migrate ArtAgents to the final elegant folderized architecture. Canonical public concepts are only orchestrators, executors, and elements. Executors are concrete runnable tools; performers/instruments are old wording and should not remain public. Orchestrators coordinate workflows; conductors are old wording and should not remain public. Every executor should live in one self-contained folder under artagents/executors/<executor_id>/ with executor.yaml, SKILL.md, implementation entrypoint such as run.py or src/, and colocated requirements/assets/guides as needed. Every orchestrator should live similarly under artagents/orchestrators/<orchestrator_id>/ with orchestrator.yaml, SKILL.md, and runtime. VibeComfy and Moirae must move from artagents/performers/curated into canonical executor folders preserving their current SKILL.md content. Current executor facade files were partially rewritten from performers; finish or correct that. Do a similar cleanup for orchestrators currently backed by conductors/curated. Remove compatibility shims generally: no public performers/conductors/instruments aliases, no top-level executor wrapper modules in artagents when implementation lives under an executor folder, no curated performer/conductor folders. Keep only necessary root/bin command entrypoints. Update registries, CLI, docs, onboarding, and tests to canonical names and paths. Keep list/inspect/doctor bootstrap-light. Validate with focused tests and canonical CLI commands.

        Idea:
Migrate ArtAgents to the final elegant folderized architecture. Canonical public concepts are only orchestrators, executors, and elements. Executors are concrete runnable tools; performers/instruments are old wording and should not remain public. Orchestrators coordinate workflows; conductors are old wording and should not remain public. Every executor should live in one self-contained folder under artagents/executors/<executor_id>/ with executor.yaml, SKILL.md, implementation entrypoint such as run.py or src/, and colocated requirements/assets/guides as needed. Every orchestrator should live similarly under artagents/orchestrators/<orchestrator_id>/ with orchestrator.yaml, SKILL.md, and runtime. VibeComfy and Moirae must move from artagents/performers/curated into canonical executor folders preserving their current SKILL.md content. Current executor facade files were partially rewritten from performers; finish or correct that. Do a similar cleanup for orchestrators currently backed by conductors/curated. Remove compatibility shims generally: no public performers/conductors/instruments aliases, no top-level executor wrapper modules in artagents when implementation lives under an executor folder, no curated performer/conductor folders. Keep only necessary root/bin command entrypoints. Update registries, CLI, docs, onboarding, and tests to canonical names and paths. Keep list/inspect/doctor bootstrap-light. Validate with focused tests and canonical CLI commands.

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: scripts/gen_effect_registry.py\n@@\n-PACKAGE_SRC = WORKSPACE_ROOT / \"packages\" / \"timeline-composition\" / \"typescript\" / \"src\"\n+PACKAGE_SRC = Path(\n+    os.environ.get(\n+        \"ARTAGENTS_TIMELINE_COMPOSITION_SRC\",\n+        str(WORKSPACE_ROOT / \"packages\" / \"timeline-composition\" / \"typescript\" / \"src\"),\n+    )\n+)\n*** Update File: tests/test_effects_catalog.py\n@@\n WORKSPACE_ROOT = ROOT.parent\n-PACKAGE_SRC = WORKSPACE_ROOT / \"packages\" / \"timeline-composition\" / \"typescript\" / \"src\"\n+PACKAGE_SRC = Path(\n+    os.environ.get(\n+        \"ARTAGENTS_TIMELINE_COMPOSITION_SRC\",\n+        str(WORKSPACE_ROOT / \"packages\" / \"timeline-composition\" / \"typescript\" / \"src\"),\n+    )\n+)\n*** Add File: tests/conftest.py\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+import atexit\n+import os\n+import shutil\n+import tempfile\n+from pathlib import Path\n+\n+\n+if \"ARTAGENTS_TIMELINE_COMPOSITION_SRC\" not in os.environ:\n+    _package_src = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"artagents-timeline-composition-src-\"))\n+    os.environ[\"ARTAGENTS_TIMELINE_COMPOSITION_SRC\"] = str(_package_src)\n+    atexit.register(lambda: shutil.rmtree(_package_src, ignore_errors=True))\n*** End Patch\n"
}
